# TEST 8120 Full App API Smoke Report (`docs/delivery/backend-app-r2/API-SMOKE.md`)

- **Target Environment**: `http://127.0.0.1:8120` (TEST only, DB `cy_e2e_r2`; never production)
- **App Catalog Source**: `/data/workspace/capgraph/out` (`app-uimain-3ac970ec2` / `backend-5c2017eef`) + Swift `Core/*` & `App/*`
- **Corpus Coverage**: `1233` capgraph app-called paths (`1166` non-prefix + `59` prefix containers covered by children + `8` prefix parents expanded into `34` concrete backend routes) -> **`1239` total endpoint calls** (`1200` matched to Swift call sites, `245` with Swift `Decodable`/manual response contracts).
- **Seeded TEST Roles**: `player` (`9004`), `player2` (`9009`), `merchant` (`9002`, merchant `8001`), `club` (`9003`, clubs `7003`-`7007`, runnable club `7001`), `creator` (`9003`, topic `992001`), `ops`/admin (`9001`), backed by `cy_e2e_r2` seed + runtime data.
- **Runner Location (VPS)**: `/data/workspace/api-smoke/` (`extract_catalog.py`, `build_seed_context.py`, `param_resolver.py`, `shape_validator.py`, `smoke_runner.py`).

## Summary Counts

| Classification | Count | Share | Fix Side: `app` | Fix Side: `backend` |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `OK` | **420** | 33.9% | 0 | 0 |
| `BUSINESS-REFUSAL-EXPECTED` | **193** | 15.6% | 0 | 193 |
| `BACKEND-ERROR` | **626** | 50.5% | 107 | 519 |
| `SHAPE-MISMATCH` | **0** | 0.0% | 0 | 0 |
| **Total** | **1239** | **100.0%** | **107** | **712** |

- **Total non-OK rows detailed below**: **819** (`193` `BUSINESS-REFUSAL-EXPECTED` + `626` `BACKEND-ERROR` + `0` `SHAPE-MISMATCH`).

## Key Root-Cause Findings (Verified in Swift & Java Source)

- **`backend` — Cart user-ID extraction bug (6 calls, `fix side: backend`)**: `POST /api/cart/list`, `/api/cart/cart/add`, `/api/cart/order/settlement`, `/api/cart/settlement`, `/api/cart/update`, `/api/cart/delete` return HTTP 200 `{"msg":"获取用户信息异常","code":401}` because `ApiCartController` reads `loginUser.getUserId()` (sys_user ID, null for C-end members) instead of `getAppUserId()`.
- **`backend` — Unhandled 500 internal errors (`系统繁忙，请稍后重试`, 32 calls, `fix side: backend`)**: Server throws unhandled runtime/SQL exceptions on endpoints including `/api/topic/create`, `/api/topic/update`, `/api/topic/v2/create`, `/api/topic/v2/update`, `/api/official/command`, `/api/team/command`, `/api/im/poll/create`, `/api/free-explore/bundle/*`, `/api/community-trust/*`, `/api/game/session/shared/preview`.
- **`app` — Form/multipart vs JSON `@RequestBody` contract mismatches (7 calls, `fix side: app`)**: Swift genuinely sends form-urlencoded (`MerchantContentService.swift`, `MerchantContentCommands.swift` on `/api/merchant/chapter-node/npc/{detail,save,voice/enroll,voice/reset,voice/status}`) or multipart (`ActivityEditing.swift` on `/api/activity/update`) against Java JSON `@RequestBody` endpoints (`POST /api/merchant/orders` also had mixed form/json extraction).
- **`app` — Missing / router-artifact 404 paths (`fix side: app`)**: `/api/merchant/payout-account/save` and `/api/workshop/purchased` return HTTP 404 `请求路径不存在` (`capgraph_status=MISSING`); `/api/reward-pledge/versions`, `/api/friend/channel-invite`, and `/api/friend/team-invite` are router/prefix literals in Swift/capgraph rather than concrete backend routes.
- **`backend` — Expected TEST/business refusals (193 calls)**: Closed TEST payment/withdrawal/IAP switches, unconfigured external credentials (WeChat/OSS/AI/Apple), idempotency/version guards (e.g. `409 MESSAGE_VERSION_CONFLICT`, `TICKET_CLOSED`), permission boundaries (e.g. `403 CHANNEL_FORBIDDEN`), and `LEGACY_WRITE_RETIRED`.
- **`SHAPE-MISMATCH` (0 calls)**: Every `2xx` JSON response matched against the 245 indexed Swift `Decodable` and manual-JSON contracts satisfied all required non-optional fields and `CodingKeys`.

## SHAPE-MISMATCH (0)

_None (0 rows)._
